No invented blue-white colour, fixed eight-point count or fan lifetime remains. **GAP:** exact pooled-instance reuse/RNG ordering remains unresolved (searched `10065807/10060935/10052182/10051bca/10051e5a`); native chooses count on visual initialization, while our transient actor representation chooses at spawn. The update also starts FX44/45 at the final corner (`10051e5a`); these secondary effects are not reproduced by this fix.
  * **Weather camera shake [CODE, fresh RE]**: GC100be767 merges queued strength×2 into its quake timer, draws three offsets `(rand()%100−50)*0.5/300 * time_left/2` before decrementing the timer, and writes camera+0x1d4. Constants GC10155f00=double0.5,1015d640=double300, ctor duration2. N3 locality callback1001ff2f receives camera+0xa4 and adds member+0x130 to the visual eye before `VisualCamera::SetPosition`; rotation and movement remain untouched. Nearby lightning GC100b9850 sends module9/0xbf only below25m, with payload `(25,1.5−distance/25*1.5,0)` at the camera; registration100b68ad→receiver100b29a0→100bda8c queues the maximum strength for the next frame. Live flow now routes this independently of audio availability. Regression: `lightning_uses_quake_timer_and_native_camera_draws`.
  * **UNRESOLVED**: per-area weather (`FUN_100be576/70b`, area positions+0x48..+0x50 and radius+0x58) remains a separate parser/blending workstream; this audio/camera cutover does not claim to resolve it. The C `rand()` stream (wind walk `FUN_100bf729`, quake, rain `FUN_100b964d`) is shared by the whole native process; this module's seeded LCG is deterministic, not a captured whole-client RNG state. No consumers were found for `f[10]`, `f[11]`, `f[13]` (struct offsets+0x28/+0x2c/+0x34 in `FUN_100be767`); `Preferences` byte+3 wind-walk gate remains default-assumed-on. Earlier claims that game day, lightning sound and f[12] camera shake were entirely absent are superseded by the live paths documented above.

### Interactive music `anarchy.sws` (`sws.rs`, `music.rs`)
`serialize.dll` ObjectArchive (@0x10004d29): `u32 version = 1; u32 nSymbols (59); nSymbols × (cstr type tag, cstr name); u32 nObjects (27144); u32 blob length + blob; nObjects × (u32 len + message)`; message = `u32 1; u32 nEntries; entries { varint symbol; u32 type; u32 elemSize; u32 totalBytes; data }`. Objects: `CProject` (1: `lid/lname/lpause/lvol/samp`, **91 layers**), `CSampleExtras` (1257 samples: layer, file stem `name`, `endmtime` ms, `entflg`, `vol`, `trans`, `ptrans`), `CTransition` (4620: `sampid[from,to]`, `fot` ms = position in the sample where the next stream starts, `ftime` ms = cross-fade length, `pri`), `PauseTrack_c` (26), `CFadeInfo` (20994, all sampled linear), `CChord` (246 markers). Layer `x\y` plays files `music/env/x/y/<name>.{wav,mp3,ogg}` (`CProject::GetAbsoluteSampleName` SIM @0x100041b7; matched case-insensitively; 20 Adonis `EP_01` samples have a differently named directory on disk: `adonis_mighty_castle`, they stay silent). `sim.config` (`LoadDirectoryCfg` SIM @0x10004b71) is optional and absent.
Playback (`SIMPlayer`, state machine `FUN_1000a661`): entry sample = uniform among `entflg` samples (`GetEntrySample` @0x100033b9); next sample = weighted random over authored transitions (`FUN_10008c7e`: `r = rand() % Σw`, choose while `r ≤ w`; unchosen candidates gain 1, reset past 999). Transitions ahead of `fot − 10 ms` into the requested layer take priority, keeping only the earliest `fot`; otherwise own-layer edges are used in force mode 0. **Default force mode is 1**, not 0: Sandy constructor @0x10002b64 writes word `0x0100` at `+0x10c` (mute byte 0, force byte `+0x10d` **1**); `PlayMusic` @0x100062e4 passes that byte to `SignalEvent`. With no authored target-layer edge, `FUN_10008d2e` picks the target entry and checks `position < old.end − min(2000, old.end−(old.end>>2)−10, target.end−(target.end>>2)−10) − 10`. If early enough, mode 1 uses `CTransition` constructor @0x1000a90e unmodified: **`fot = old.endmtime − target.totoffms`, `ftime = target.totoffms`, `fit = 0`**. The 2000 ms bound is eligibility, not a fixed fade duration; modes other than 1 rewrite fades through `FUN_10008bd8`. Late requests fall back to an own-layer edge and reconsider on the next sample. Thus battle layers need no district exit edges: the default forced transition returns to district music after combat clears. `SetCombatMusicMode` is unrelated (no-op, below).

**Pause tracks** (`lpause`, `PauseTracker` @0x10002712/0x1000289f/0x10002761): nodes store play/pause beats, fade times and `loopto`; forest\Day = 120 s music then 600 s silence. A request does not reset the pause clock. It resets on `Play(..,true)` and when a different-layer stream starts (`FUN_100096b6`). A span-ending `ptrans` plays its closing sample; otherwise the node fade closes the current stream. `Pause(false,false)` saves the pending target; after silence `Play(saved,false)` resumes without clock reset. Explicit force-0 unit tests retain the unforced old-layer/pause-clock behavior; it is not the shipped default. Final gain = `Total_Music · layer lvol · sample vol · fade`.

**No successor [CODE+DATA]**: `FUN_10009acd` schedules sample end in state 5 when transition selection returns NULL; `FUN_10009529` moves 5 → **6 (silent)**, and `FUN_1000a661` has no state-6 update case. This must not automatically restart an entry sample. Sandy `PlayMusic` @0x100062e4 skips an unchanged substituted layer (`this+0x74`), so 1 Hz calls cannot restart state 6; a changed request reaches `SignalEvent` @0x1000a599 and starts the new entry. Real `NeutralVictory02` has no authored transitions; the explicit force-0 regression checks its exhaustion separately from default-force battle exit. Transition selection does not filter missing files; open failure belongs to the stream state machine.

**Combat mode wrappers [CODE]**: Gamecode @0x100111e1 forwards to Sandy module `SetCombatMusicMode` @0x10007315; that checks the interface pointer and invokes `SandyInterface_t::SetCombatMusicMode` @0x100021be, whose implementation is only `return`. Ignoring `CombatEvent::CombatMusic` is intentional. The stuck-battle root cause was incorrectly treating Sandy's default force byte as 0, not a missing stop operation or a missing victory snapshot.

### District ambience [CODE+DATA]
`FUN_100bd3d9` (GC @0x100bd3d9) registers `SM_Sandy_Env_Background{Night,Dawn,Day,Dusk}_<N>`, `N = district.music_ids[0]`; `FUN_100ba221` plays the layers of the current district every frame, non-positional, with a trapezoid level over the game time `t` (seconds, 0..97200): night (0, 0, dawnS−K, dawnS), dawn (dawnS−K, dawnS, dawnE−K, dawnE), day (dawnE−K, dawnE, duskS−K, duskS), dusk (duskS−K, duskS, duskE−K, duskE), `K = 300 s` (@GC 0x1015d640), `t` forced to 1 once `t > duskE − K`; ramp-up `(t−A)/(B−A)`, plateau B..C, ramp-down `1 − (t−C)/(D−C)`. The wav per id/period is in the sbf record (103 ids; 566 → id 37; 1 forest_bg_*, 3 jungle, 4 swamp, 5/7/11/26/27 city_clan_*, 8 ocean_bg_01, 12/13 dungeon_bg/catacombs_bg, 14–18 env_wasteland_*, **0 = dungeon_bg_01 + catacombs_bg_01/02**: 1969 of 4241 districts have id 0, and the client code has no skip for 0 so they play the catacomb set [as per code]). Our layers loop (the sbf duration is the 1 s keep-alive); a district change retires the old layers (hold 1 s, fade 4 s). Level = trapezoid · `vol_max (0.498)` · `Total_FX`.

### Statel sound emitters (`Report::sounds`, `Runtime::tick_emitters`)
The 18-byte entries of the statel file's second list (see *Statel file*): `f32 x,y,z; u32 sound id; u16 radius`. N3 `n3StatelController_t::RunFunction` (@0x100260d2) runs `EvaluateStatelSoundFog` (@0x10024eff) every frame with the camera eye: inside `radius` the first frame arms the emitter, from the second frame `StatelSoundRun` (@0x10024e97) calls `PlayGameSound(id, xyz, dur 2.0, vol 1.0, 0, 0, 100, 1, radius)` **every frame** → a *looping* sound (duration override > 0) whose level is `attenuation(d, radius) · vol · Total_FX` and which fades out `fade_out` s after a 2 s hold once the camera left. We keep a looping voice per emitter, update its gain every frame, apply the `prob` roll once per second, and handle children by the flags above (`random_child` re-picked when the child ends, `play_all` at start, else timed). 1362 emitters / 55 ids over all playfields (566: turbines, dishes, `whoom_pha_loop` + random electric noises; 127: steam random children, `fire_camp_loop`). Test `statel_emitters_start_inside_radius_and_stop_after_leaving` (566 turbine r 25). A call whose `AllocateChannel` found no handle (pool full, nothing stealable) is repeated every frame while the camera is inside (`emitter_retries_when_the_pool_is_full`); ambience children are first armed with their own interval (`ambience_children_first_arm_uses_their_own_interval`).

### Combat music [CODE] (`combat.rs`, `Audio::set_combat_sample`)
Ported 1:1 from `SandyInterface_t` (SandyInterface.dll): `CombatUpdate` @0x10005c42, `Frameprocess` @0x10003f61, `ProcessCombatMusic` @0x10001eef, `LoadSimProject` @0x10005ffa (table), `PlayMusic` @0x100062e4, `SetCombatMusicOverride` @0x100021c1, `SetStaticBattleMusicMode` @0x100019fc (`m_nCombatMusicPreferences`, DLL default 0; the GUI pref `BattlemusicMode` (`SlotPrefBattlemusicModeChanged` GUI @0x100c4862) default **3** sets it; `Prefs::battlemusic_mode`). `SetCombatMusicMode`/`SetDeathMusicMode` are empty stubs.
* **Feed** (`Gamecode FUN_10059736`, called by the character `Run` `FUN_1005b016` @vtable 0x1015fabc, i.e. **every frame for every character**; ctor values: own health `+0x78` = previous `+0xac` = 100, rest 0): `n3Dynel+0x140` = "is the client control dynel" (`GetClientControlDynel`, set by `FUN_1005c0f3`/`FUN_10077af2`/`SetMainDynel` @0x10019f77). Controlled char with MaxHealth (stat 1) ≠ 0 → `CombatUpdate(isSelf=1, …)`; with MaxHealth = 0 nothing. Any other char needs MaxHealth ≠ 0 and a fight controller (`+0x1d4`) whose state `+0x44` ≠ 1 (fighting): if its fight target (`controller+0x4c`, identity type 50000, `FUN_10058e36`) is the controlled char (**it attacks us**) → `CombatUpdate(0, a=its health %, …)`; if it targets anyone else it only reports its death (`Life == 0` → `a = 0`, else nothing). Arguments: `a = trunc(Life(27)·100 / MaxHealth(1,kind 3))` (`_ftol`), ≤ 100, forced to 1 when `Life > 0`; `b` = stat 421 (0x1a5; stored, **never read** by any decision); `side` = stat 33 (0 neutral, 1 clan, 2 omni; anything else → 0); `id` = `dynel+0x18`; `flag` = byte `dynel+0x21c`; `level` = stat 54. **Enemy = the strongest character fighting us**: the accumulator (`+0x8c..0xa0`) takes a sample with `a ≠ 0` and a higher level, or the same level and more health (`b` only with a level upgrade); a sample with `id == last processed opponent id (+0xa4)` and `a == 0` records "that opponent is dead" (health 0).
* **Timer** (`Frameprocess`, game-frame dt from `Timer_t`): `if (+0xc0 > 0) +0xc0 -= dt; if (+0xc0 <= 0) ProcessCombatMusic; count(+0xa8) = 0`. Locks (floats @0x1000a26c/70/74): **rebirth 25 s, death 5 s, victory 20 s**; every other state has no lock (re-evaluated every frame).
* **`ProcessCombatMusic`**: `count == 0` or pref 0 → state 0 (no tail). Own health vs the previous processing's: **dead→alive = rebirth `side+1`** (25 s), **alive→dead = death `side+4`** (5 s); else fight evaluation: `count < 2` (no opponent sample) → 0; `r` = level ratio in % (own `+0x84`, opponent `+0x9c`): own<opp `200 − 100·own/opp` (101..200), own>opp `100·opp/own` (0..99), equal 100; **`r < 25` → 0, `r < 100` needs pref 3, `r < 150` needs pref ≥ 2, `r ≥ 150` pref ≥ 1**; then if `r ≥ 100` or the opponent flag `+0xa0` is 0: opponent had health at the last processing and has none now → **victory `side+7`** (20 s); else `d = own% − opp%`: `|d| ≤ 29` → 10, `30..59` → 11 (+1 if r>133), `≥60` → 11 (+2 if r>133), `−59..−30` → 14 (+1), `≤−60` → 14 (+2) (so Winning: 11 Medium / 12 SlightlyBig / 13 GreatlyBig, Losing 14/15/16); `r < 100` with the flag set → 10. Tail: `+0xac = own%`, own% = 0 (re-set by the next self sample), if `count ≥ 2` `last id/last opp health = current`, opponent health/level cleared. A processing frame without a self sample therefore sees own health 0 (= "dead").
* **Table** (`LoadSimProject` fills `this+0xc8..0x104` with `FindLayerID`; all 16 exist in `anarchy.sws`, tested): 1 `battle\NeutralRebirth`, 2 `ClanRebirth`, 3 `OmniRebirth`, 4 `NeutralDeath`, 5 `ClanDeath`, 6 `OmniDeath`, 7/8/9 `NeutralVictory` (all three), 10 `battle\Neutral`, 11 `WinningMedium`, 12 `WinningSlightlyBig`, 13 `WinningGreatlyBig`, 14 `LosingMedium`, 15 `LosingSlightlyBig`, 16 `LosingBadlyBig`.
* **Playing** (`PlayMusic(layer)` runs only from `SetupENVSounds` @0x1000657d ← `ActivateEnvZone` @0x10007383 = the 1 Hz district module, and `PlayStartupMusic`): state 1..16 → layer = `SetCombatMusicOverride` layer (`FindLayerID`; not found / 0 → table) else the table layer (not found = −1 = silence); state 0 → the requested district layer. So a state change is audible at the next district evaluation (≤ 1 s) and the district layer comes back the same way. `Audio::update(dt, …)` runs `combat.update(dt)` first, then the 1 Hz evaluation; `Audio::set_music_layer` also applies the substitution.
* **API for the app**: per game frame, for each character build `ao_audio::CharInfo { is_local, life, max_health, fighting, target_is_local, side, id, flag, level, metric }` and call `audio.set_combat_char(&info)` (= `char_sample` → `set_combat_sample`, a faithful `FUN_10059736`), then `audio.update(dt, cam, day)` once. `audio.combat_state()` (0..16), `audio.set_combat_music_override(Some("EP_01\\Battle_new"))` (tweak `CombatMusicOverride`), `Prefs { battlemusic_mode }` via `set_prefs`. Own char: `is_local` = the controlled char, `fighting` = fight controller state ≠ 1 (our fight state, e.g. `ao_net` Attack/StopFight), `target_is_local` = the opponent's current target is us.
* **Resolved**: *who the enemy is* (strongest fighter targeting us, above); *`b`* (stat 421, unused); *`side`* (stat 33, only selects the Rebirth/Death/Victory variant: rebirth/death/victory of the *local* char, `side` of the local char); *timers*; *update frequency* (every frame). **Unresolved**: `dynel+0x21c` — written 0 by `ClearFlag` (vtable 0x1015fb34, FUN_10058415) when state-flag bit `0x10000000` of `Flags` (+0x134) is cleared and in the dynel reset (`FUN_1005cb6a`); the setter was not found (no `MOV/OR/SET*` to `+0x21c` in Gamecode/N3 other than zero stores); consumers: the char `Run` (`FUN_1005b016`) skips the walking/NPC update when set and calls a `CharVehicle`/`NPCVehicle` vtable +0x94 hook, so it is most likely "attached to a vehicle" **[INFERENCE]**. For the combat music it only matters for an opponent with `r < 100` (weaker): set → always `battle\Neutral`. The flag source must be fed by the app; leave `false` until the Flags bit 28 semantics are known. Whether a combat state applies without a district (no `ActivateEnvZone` call) is not known [INFERENCE: it applies].

### Login / character-select / creation audio and prefs [CODE]
* **Login, server select and character select are silent**: `LoginModule_c::SlotInitialize` (GUI @0x10012204) only calls `StopAllSoundEffects`; `ServerLogin3DModule_t` plays nothing. Command/button sound ids (`Command_t` @0x10017783 parses `SM…` tokens, `InvisibleButton_t::InvSendMessage` @0x1001f3cd) exist in the GUI code, but no command definition of the shipped client carries one (hash scan of every string of all DLLs/exe/gui/twk/text against the 6895 sbf ids), and `PlayGUISound*` are empty stubs. The 94 generic records under `sfx/gui/` (`NNN.wav`, `if_*`, `gui_door_*`, `nextback`, `cc/ah/*`) are referenced by **neither name nor number** anywhere in the client (little-endian scan of every non-sound file incl. DLLs/exe/gui/twk/text/data for the ids; no sound attributes in the gui xml; brute force of all 467 680 identifier-like words of all DLLs/exe/gui/twk with 17 prefixes found only two accidental hash collisions, `SM_Sandy_GUI_MENU_BUTTON` → `011.wav` and `SM_Sandy_Game_XPRO` → `005.wav`, neither string exists in the client): unused in this build (reachable at most through server driven `PlaySoundCommand` messages carrying an id). Named UI sounds are the `SM_Sandy_CC_*` set plus `SM_Sandy_Gui_Mission_Complete[_Clan|_Omni|_Neutral]` and `SM_Sandy_Game_Level_Complete*`.
* **Startup music**: only `CharacterLoggedInMessage` (GUI @0x10016d30, when the server confirms the chosen character) with `SoundOnOff && MusicOn` calls `PlayStartupMusic` = layer **`mountain\night`** (SI @0x10006554) until the first district update replaces it (`Audio::play_startup_music`).
* **Leaving world audio**: Sandy `StopAllSounds` @0x10007446 calls `StopAllSoundEffects`, `StopMusic`, and clears the district sound id. `StopMusic` @0x1000221e calls `SIMPlayer::Stop(true,true)`, destroys/nulls the player, clears `SE_SetSIMPointer`, resets layer fields `+0x5c/+0x74` to −1 and state fields `+0x94/+0x108` to 0; `DeinitializeSandy` @0x100029e4 also calls `StopMusic` and `ReleaseAllSamples`. This is a hard lifecycle stop, not `PlayMusic(None)` (which lets the current sample finish). The port's shared `Play::show_login` calls `Audio::reset`: streams, pending transitions/pause resumes, combat snapshots, playfield/ambience/emitters, keep-alives, held voices and delayed effect commands are cleared; mixer voices stop immediately, while sound preferences/output gain remain intact. `/camp` completes before world updates so that the closing frame cannot enqueue fresh playback. Subsequent login frames may still tick the audio engine but cannot restart the old district or delayed commands; no login music layer is requested. Regressions: `reset_closes_streams_and_cancels_pause_resume`, `reset_clears_world_commands_but_keeps_sound_preferences`, `world_audio_reset_stays_silent_until_character_login`, and `leaving_the_world_closes_chat_and_interact`.
  The logout trigger is directly traced: `ActivateGameClosing(2)` (GUI @0x10028194) starts the nine-frame return; `FlowControlModule_t::FrameProcess` (GUI @0x10028a9e, return branch @0x10028b80) emits `GlobalSignals +0x28c` when the closing frames expire. `SoundOptionsMonitor_c` ctor binds that signal to `SlotResetAudio` (GUI @0x100c4e7f–0x100c4e8d), whose thunk @0x100c47c9 calls `StopAllSounds` through import @0x101a7ef8. No login music is requested.
* **Character creation** (`CharCreateModule_t`): `InitialiseMessage` (@0x1011ba81) `GUISoundInitialisation` (parses `SM_Sandy_Gui.sbf`); `RunIntro` (@0x1011b972) `SM_Sandy_CC_Opening_Music` once; `FrameProcess` (@0x1011c02c) `SM_Sandy_CC_Ambience` **every frame** (keep-alive: duration 1 s, fade-out 4 s; `Audio::play_ui_keepalive`). Every scene button: mouse-over `SM_Sandy_CC_GUI_Mouseover`, press `SM_Sandy_CC_GUI_Select` (`SceneBase_t` @0x10122ee7); scene starts: Breed `StopAllSoundEffects` + `SM_Sandy_CC_Select_Breed` (@0x10113379), breed button → `SM_Sandy_CC_{Atrox,Opifex_Male,Opifex_Female,Nano_Male,Nano_Female,Solitus_Male,Solitus_Female}`, Appearance `SM_Sandy_CC_Appearance`, Profession `SM_Sandy_CC_Select_Profession` and per-profession `SM_Sandy_CC_{Adventurer,Agent,Bureaucrat,Doctor,Enforcer,Engineer,Fixer,Keeper,MA,Meta_Selected,Nanotech_Selected,Shade,Soldier,Trader}`, `GUI_Error` when Next is pressed without a profession, Name `SM_Sandy_CC_Name_Character`. All non-positional at `vol_max (0.498) · Total_FX`. `SM_Sandy_CC_Nick/Name` are not in the bank (silent). `Audio::play_ui(name)` plays any of them (exact sbf name; `GUI_Select` also resolves via the `SM_Sandy_CC_`/`SM_Sandy_Gui_` prefixes).
* **Prefs** (`SoundOptionsMonitor_c`, GUI @0x100c4cc9/0x100c4ace; defaults in `cd_image/gui/Default/LoginPrefs.xml` + `MainPrefs.xml`): `SoundOnOff true` (false deinitialises Miles), `MasterVolume 1.0`, `SoundFXOn true`, `FXVolume 1.0`, `MusicOn true` (false also `SetMusicMute`), `MusicVolume 1.0`, `BattlemusicMode 3` (`Prefs::battlemusic_mode`, combat music gating 0..3), `VoiceSndFxOn true`, `VoiceSndFxVolume 1.0` (player voice files only). `Total_FX = master·FX·mutes` (ambience, weather, emitters and UI sounds all use it; **there is no separate ambience volume**), `Total_Music = master·Music·mutes`. `Audio::set_prefs(&Prefs)` (defaults = the shipped ones; test `weather_slot_prefs_and_keepalive` checks the music mute).

### Implementation map
`decode.rs` (symphonia + RIFF-MP3), `mixer.rs` (voices, streams, fades, 13-handle priority pool, stats), `engine.rs` (cpal CoreAudio output, `Audio`: `start`/`offline`/`update`/`set_playfield`/`set_music_layer`/`play_startup_music`/`play_ui`/`play_ui_keepalive`/`set_prefs`/`set_weather`/`status`), `combat.rs` (combat music state machine, see *Combat music*), `game.rs` (`Library` = sws + both sbf, `PlayfieldAudio::load(store, id, &report.sounds)`, district music, ambience, emitters), `music.rs` (SIMPlayer), `sws.rs`/`sbf.rs`/`district.rs` (parsers). Viewer: `aomac view pf <id>` starts audio unless `--mute`/`--screenshot`; `ao_render::run_viewer_hooked` calls the hook with camera position and dt each frame (listener = camera, day clock = start + dt · `--time-scale`). `AOMAC_AUDIO_LOG=1` prints the mixer status every 2 s, e.g. `audio[MacBook Pro Speakers (44100 Hz, 2 ch)]: 1743 callbacks, 892416 frames, 3 voices, rms 0.0124, peak 0.801 music=Some("Dday19.wav")`. Offline tests render the mixer into a buffer (`tests/mix.rs`: resample + level, fades, voice cap, 3-stream cap, WAV decode + stream voice RMS; `tests/game_real.rs`: real client chain) — no device needed.

**Gaps**: weather's remaining scalar/area/RNG questions are listed under *Weather*; `sim.config` (absent file); the setter/meaning of `dynel+0x21c` (combat music flag, see *Combat music*); the20 Adonis `EP_01` samples whose directory differs on disk; per-object sound coverage follows the network/animation layers. Authored traffic engine, landing and thrust sounds now run in the live and offline viewer; this does not add the intentionally unsupported non-playfield/integrating traffic templates.


### Planet map (`ao_formats::planetmap`, `textures/PlanetMap`)
Index text (`Name`, `Type Rubika|Shadowlands`, `CoordsFile`, then per zoom level `File`, `TextureSize 128`, `Size`, `Tiles tx ty`, `MapRect l t r b`, `tx*ty` `FilePos`); parser `FUN_1004cb0c` 0x1004cb0c. Default index has 2 levels (10×11 and 31×37 tiles, level 1 = 4× level 0), tiles are PNG/JPEG blobs in one `.bin`, **column-major** (tile `i` = column `i/ty`, row `i%ty`; row-major gives a scrambled canvas). Coordinates XML (`FUN_1004c008` 0x1004c008): `<Playfield id x z xscale zscale>`. Marker (`FUN_1004b81a` 0x1004b81a): `gx = x_attr + pos.x`, `gz = z_attr + pos.z`; `px = (gx-31022)*(r-l)/18751*xscale + l`; `py = (b-t) - (t + (gz-24880)*(b-t)/24115*zscale)` (doubles at 0x101b1258/50/48/40); verified: Newland City lands on its label. Survey: `cargo run --release -p ao-formats --example planet_png -- <client> Normal/PlanetMapIndexNormal.txt 0 out.png`.

### Playfield map image (`ao_formats::topdown`)
CPU top-down raster of `Report::ground` (terrain / room shells / water instances, after the sky objects): up-facing opaque triangles, highest wins, unlit texels; per-pixel `owner` = room instance. Equivalent of `PFMapGroundRenderer_c` (0x101b0a78). UNRESOLVED: the original's map lighting. Survey: example `pf_map_png`.
